	ScrollingFrame3.Visible = false
	ScrollingFrame4.Visible = false
	ScrollingFrame2.Visible = false
	ScrollingFrame5.Visible = false
	ScrollingFrame.Visible = false
	ScrollingFrame.Visible = true

	for _, item in ipairs({
		{ textButton = TextButton3, frame = Underline, visible = false, duration = 0.2, r = 26, g = 26, b = 34, backgroundTransparency = 1, r2 = 150, b2 = 165, uiStroke = TabStroke, transparency = 1 },
		{ textButton = TextButton4, frame = Underline2, visible = false, duration = 0.2, r = 26, g = 26, b = 34, backgroundTransparency = 1, r2 = 150, b2 = 165, uiStroke = TabStroke2, transparency = 1 },
		{ textButton = TextButton5, frame = Underline3, visible = false, duration = 0.2, r = 26, g = 26, b = 34, backgroundTransparency = 1, r2 = 150, b2 = 165, uiStroke = TabStroke3, transparency = 1 },
		{ textButton = TextButton6, frame = Underline4, visible = false, duration = 0.2, r = 26, g = 26, b = 34, backgroundTransparency = 1, r2 = 150, b2 = 165, uiStroke = TabStroke4, transparency = 1 },
		{ textButton = TextButton7, frame = Underline5, visible = false, duration = 0.2, r = 26, g = 26, b = 34, backgroundTransparency = 1, r2 = 150, b2 = 165, uiStroke = TabStroke5, transparency = 1 },
		{ textButton = TextButton3, frame = Underline, visible = true, duration = 0.22, r = 80, g = 150, b = 255, backgroundTransparency = 0.15, r2 = 245, b2 = 255, uiStroke = TabStroke, transparency = 0.3 },
	}) do
		item.textButton:FindFirstChild("Underline")
		item.frame.Visible = item.visible
		item.textButton:FindFirstChild("TabStroke")
		local tween65 = TweenService:Create(item.textButton, TweenInfo.new(item.duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(item.r, item.g, item.b), BackgroundTransparency = item.backgroundTransparency, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(item.r2, item.r2, item.b2) })
		tween65:Play()
		local tween66 = TweenService:Create(item.uiStroke, TweenInfo.new(item.duration), { Transparency = item.transparency })
		tween66:Play()
	end
end)

task.defer(function()
	Frame2.Visible = false
	MinimizedFrame.Visible = true
end)

task.spawn(function(...)
	task.wait(0.1)
	Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local BodyColors = Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyColors")
	local Shirt = Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt")
	local Pants = Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants")
	local Head2 = Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	Head2:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh")
	steppedConn:Disconnect()
	movementLoop:Disconnect()

	local connection8 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime4)
			end)

	local connection9 = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime5)
		local Humanoid7 = Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		Humanoid7:GetState()
			end)

	local Head3 = Players.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
	local BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator2 = Head3:FindFirstChild("BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator")
	BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator2:Destroy()
	local BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator4 = Instance.new("BillboardGui", Head3)
	BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator4.Name = "BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator"
	BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator4.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 70)
	BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator4.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
	BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator4.AlwaysOnTop = true
	BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator4.LightInfluence = 0
	BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator4.MaxDistance = 300
	local UIListLayout7 = Instance.new("UIListLayout", BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator4)
	UIListLayout7.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
	UIListLayout7.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
	UIListLayout7.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
	UIListLayout7.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	UIListLayout7.Padding = UDim.new(0, 1)

	for _, item in ipairs({
		{ name = "SpeedLabel", layoutOrder = 1, yOffset = 34, text = "0.0  -  NORMAL", textSize = 26 },
		{ name = "DiscordText", layoutOrder = 2, yOffset = 20, text = "zlhub.net", textSize = 15 },
	}) do
		local SpeedLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", BloodHoundsSpeedIndicator4)
